§11.4.173 — Containerized + distributed build mandate (User mandate, 2026-06-29). EVERY build of EVERY component (source compile, artifact/package/installer/container-image production, codegen, asset render — for ANY language/platform: Go, Android/Gradle, desktop, web, native, firmware) MUST run INSIDE a specialized build container provisioned via the digital.vasic.containers submodule (§11.4.76) — NEVER on the bare host. The build containers MUST be DISTRIBUTED to the designated remote build host(s) via the SAME containers-submodule distribution mechanism (remote compose over SSH §11.4.76, rootless §11.4.161) so the build EXECUTES on the remote host (offloading the developer/main host); once complete the produced artifacts MUST be brought BACK to the originating host (scp/rsync/volume copy) for use/flashing/distribution. Building outside a container, or on the bare host, is FORBIDDEN — a release blocker (the “works on my machine” / unreproducible-build class). Build-host target + build-container definitions are config-injected (§11.4.28, never hardcoded in the submodule); a missing build-container capability is added by EXTENDING the containers submodule upstream (§11.4.74), never an ad-hoc host build. Honest boundary (§11.4.6): the containerized + distributed build guarantees reproducibility + host-isolation + capacity offload — it does NOT replace the §11.4.40 full-suite retest, §11.4.108 artifact→runtime verification, or §11.4.38 installable-asset evidence on the brought-back artifact. Classification: universal (§11.4.17). Composes §11.4.76 / §11.4.161 / §11.4.74 / §11.4.28 / §11.4.24 / §11.4.82 / §11.4.121 / §11.4.38 / §11.4.108 / §12.6. Propagation gate CM-COVENANT-114-173-PROPAGATION (literal 11.4.173) + recommended gate CM-CONTAINERIZED-DISTRIBUTED-BUILD + paired §1.1 mutation. Canonical authority: constitution submodule Constitution.md §11.4.173. Non-compliance is a release blocker. No escape hatch — no --build-on-host, --skip-container-build, --local-build-ok, --no-distributed-build, --bare-host-build flag.

§11.4.174 — Shared-host process-ownership verification mandate (User mandate, 2026-06-29). Verbatim operator mandate: “Make sure that processes we are checking are always OURS! … on every host there may be other work being done!” On ANY host where concurrent work from other projects / users / agents may run (the normal case — a shared developer workstation), EVERY inspection of, conclusion about, or action on a process, build, daemon, port, lock-file, or system-state signal MUST FIRST positively VERIFY the target is OURS before drawing a diagnosis or acting. A diagnosis based on another project’s process is a §11.4.6 guessing error; KILLING / signalling / restarting / altering a process that is NOT positively ours is a §12 + §11.4.133 safety violation (operator-workload destruction) — forbidden. Forensic FACT (2026-06-29): a 200%-CPU java + gradlew on the shared host was nearly mis-attributed to our build but was a separate catalogizer project’s gradle test (cwd=Projects/catalogizer); a naive pgrep java/gradle would have matched it as “ours.” ALL hold: (a) positive-ownership discriminator REQUIRED — attribute to us ONLY via a strong signal: cwd resolving inside THIS project’s tree, argv naming our scripts/artifacts/repo path, a PID we launched + recorded (§11.4.147 registry), or a lock/port/socket path under our tree; a loose name match (pgrep java/gradle/node/python/podman/zig) is NEVER sufficient; (b) NEVER kill/signal/restart a process not positively ours — other projects’ daemons/pods are off-limits; the §12.8 pkill -f GradleDaemon remediation MUST be scoped to OUR daemons (cwd/argv under our tree), never host-wide; (c) contention → surface, don’t break (§11.4.66 / §11.4.101), keep progressing non-blocked work; (d) every pgrep/ps filter MUST exclude the checking command’s OWN argv AND other-project matches, preferring our absolute repo path; (e) applies to ALL state (ports, lock files, caches, pods, tmux sessions, temp files), not just processes. Honest boundary (§11.4.6): ownership-verified inspection prevents mis-attribution + cross-project damage — it does not itself prove our process is healthy. Classification: universal (§11.4.17). Composes §11.4.6 / §11.4.101 / §11.4.66 / §11.4.147 / §12 / §12.8 / §11.4.133. Propagation gate CM-COVENANT-114-174-PROPAGATION (literal 11.4.174) + recommended gate CM-PROCESS-OWNERSHIP-VERIFIED + paired §1.1 mutation. Canonical authority: constitution submodule Constitution.md §11.4.174. Non-compliance is a release blocker. No escape hatch — no --assume-process-ours, --loose-pgrep-ok, --kill-any-matching-process, --skip-ownership-check flag.

§11.4.176 — Conflict-free multi-track work-division + exactly-once claim registry + capability-aware deadlock-proof device-lock (User mandate, 2026-07-02). When a project runs multiple concurrent work-STREAM tracks (§11.4.167 / §11.4.58 / §11.4.103) sharing (a) a common pool of workable items and (b) a scarce pool of exclusive hardware/resources, TWO cooperating arbitration layers are MANDATORY, both conflict-free BY CONSTRUCTION: (A) work-item layer — exactly-once claim registry — every workable item OR logical GROUP of items sharing files/subsystem/feature/schema is assigned to AT MOST ONE track at a time via a lock-arbitrated append-only claim registry (extends §11.4.147 + §11.4.116); claim-before-touch, a second claim on an already-claimed item is DENIED; logical groups are claimed WHOLE (disjoint file-scope §11.4.58 L3 + per-track worktree/CoW §11.4.167 = tracks physically cannot touch the same bytes); no codebase loss (§9.2 backups / §11.4.113 ff-only-merge-onto-latest-main NEVER force-push / §11.4.84 quiescence / §11.4.41 merge-first); results ALWAYS produced (abandoned claim TTL-expires back to the pool). (B) hardware layer — capability-aware deadlock-proof device-lock — when N tracks outnumber M exclusive devices, exactly ONE track owns each device (§11.4.119); a track leases ONE OR MORE devices selected by required CAPABILITY (not merely identity); the arbiter is DEADLOCK-FREE by breaking all four Coffman conditions (hold-and-wait eliminated — multi-device acquire is ALL-OR-NOTHING in one critical section; circular-wait eliminated — acquire is NON-BLOCKING, wins the whole set or returns EBUSY; no-preemption relaxed — lease TTL + heartbeat + reap so a crashed holder’s lease auto-expires §11.4.147; the only lock held is a short bounded flock over the atomic check-and-claim); registry = append-only JSONL + atomically-rewritten snapshot (§11.4.116). (C) universal decoupling + evidence-based resource-tuning — both layers universal/decoupled/project-not-aware (§11.4.17/§11.4.28/§11.4.35); resource-tuning decisions (stream count, per-stream memory §12.6, lease breadth, queue depth §11.4.167) MUST be EVIDENCE-BASED (§11.4.24 / §11.4.5), never guessed (§11.4.6). Classification: universal (§11.4.17). Composes §11.4.6/§11.4.17/§11.4.24/§11.4.28/§11.4.35/§11.4.41/§11.4.58/§11.4.84/§11.4.85/§11.4.103/§11.4.113/§11.4.116/§11.4.119/§11.4.147/§11.4.167/§12.6/§9.2. Propagation gate CM-COVENANT-114-176-PROPAGATION (literal 11.4.176) + recommended gates CM-WORK-DIVISION-EXCLUSIVE-CLAIM / CM-NO-CROSS-TRACK-SCOPE-OVERLAP / CM-LOGICAL-GROUP-COHESION / CM-DEVICE-LOCK-DEADLOCK-FREE + paired §1.1 mutations. Canonical authority: constitution submodule Constitution.md §11.4.176. Non-compliance is a release blocker. No escape hatch — no --allow-cross-track-overlap, --skip-claim-registry, --split-logical-group, --blocking-device-acquire, --allow-partial-device-hold, --tune-resources-by-guess flag.

§11.4.177 — Developer-tooling project-decoupling + invocation-directory operation (User mandate, 2026-07-04). No project-specific script / hook / alias / binary may be wired — copied, symlinked, PATH-exported, or aliased — into a global or shared developer-tooling PATH; shared tooling MUST be project-agnostic and operate on the INVOCATION directory (cwd, or an explicit path/config argument), NEVER a hardcoded project path. A project-specific helper lives in + runs from its own repo; a genuinely-reusable helper is promoted to the shared toolkit ONLY after being stripped of every project-specific assumption (paths, device serials, package names, region endpoints) and taking its target from the invocation context. A project script reachable on the global/shared PATH is a decoupling violation of equal severity to importing project-specific code into a shared submodule (§11.4.28). Forensic FACT (2026-07-04): a project cwd-hook symlinked into the global Claude-Toolkit PATH re-coupled every alias to one hardcoded checkout. Honest boundary (§11.4.6): decoupling guarantees the tool is project-agnostic — NOT that it is correct (that still needs the tool’s own tests). Classification: universal (§11.4.17). Composes §11.4.28 / §11.4.29 / §11.4.35 / §11.4.11 / §11.4.6. Propagation gate CM-COVENANT-114-177-PROPAGATION (literal 11.4.177) + recommended gate CM-TOOLING-PROJECT-DECOUPLED + paired §1.1 mutation. Canonical authority: constitution submodule Constitution.md §11.4.177. Non-compliance is a release blocker. No escape hatch — no --global-symlink-ok, --hardcode-project-path, --wire-into-shared-path flag.

§11.4.178 — Track-qualified identity for parallel work streams (session-name collision ban) (User mandate, 2026-07-04). Parallel work streams that share hardware, a git backing store, a tmux/session namespace, a lock namespace, or any single-owner resource MUST be addressed by a TRACK-QUALIFIED identity (<project>__<track>__<role>), NEVER a bare directory basename. Multiple checkouts/clones/worktrees sharing basename atmosphere collapse into one session/lock/log key and cross-wire tmux targets, lock files, device claims, and log directories (the observed collision, 2026-07-04). Every registry row, session name, lock path, log directory, and device claim MUST carry the track-qualified key; a bare-basename identity for a resource with ≥ 2 concurrent claimants is a violation. Honest boundary (§11.4.6): a unique identity prevents cross-wiring — it does not itself arbitrate ownership (that is §11.4.119 + §11.4.176, the multi-track work-division + exactly-once-claim + device-lock arbitration anchor). Classification: universal (§11.4.17). Composes §11.4.111 / §11.4.119 / §11.4.176 / §11.4.29 / §11.4.58. Propagation gate CM-COVENANT-114-178-PROPAGATION (literal 11.4.178) + recommended gate CM-TRACK-QUALIFIED-IDENTITY + paired §1.1 mutation. Canonical authority: constitution submodule Constitution.md §11.4.178. Non-compliance is a release blocker. No escape hatch — no --bare-basename-ok, --skip-track-qualifier, --shared-session-name flag.

§11.4.179 — Corruption-isolated parallel git streams (own-.git independent clones, not shared-common-dir worktrees) (User mandate, 2026-07-04). When corruption-isolation is a requirement, parallel git work streams MUST each be an isolated repository with its OWN .git (own object store, own index, own lock namespace), NOT git worktree checkouts sharing one common .git. A shared common-dir is a single point of failure: one stale index.lock / .commit_all.lock / .push_all.lock freezes EVERY stream at once (the observed 9-h all-track freeze, 2026-07-04), and one corrupted object store loses every stream. Each stream’s git rev-parse --git-common-dir MUST resolve INSIDE the stream’s own tree; a common-dir resolving to a shared parent is a violation. Where CoW/reflink disk-feasibility is the constraint, use the §11.4.167 reflink-clone-with-own-.git path (a btrfs/XFS/ZFS/APFS reflink shares extents on disk while keeping a per-stream .git, so isolation and disk-feasibility both hold). Honest boundary (§11.4.6): own-.git isolation contains lock/corruption blast radius — it does NOT remove per-repo stale-lock reaping (§11.4.180) nor ff-only no-force integration (§11.4.113). Classification: universal (§11.4.17). Composes §11.4.167 / §11.4.119 / §11.4.176 / §11.4.58 / §9.2. Propagation gate CM-COVENANT-114-179-PROPAGATION (literal 11.4.179) + recommended gate CM-GIT-STREAMS-OWN-COMMON-DIR + paired §1.1 mutation. Canonical authority: constitution submodule Constitution.md §11.4.179. Non-compliance is a release blocker. No escape hatch — no --shared-common-git, --worktree-when-isolation-required, --skip-git-isolation flag.

§11.4.180 — Commit/push (single-writer) wrappers MUST auto-reap provably-stale locks before acquiring (User mandate, 2026-07-04). Every commit / push / single-writer wrapper MUST, before acquiring its own lock, auto-reap any PROVABLY-stale git lock — one whose recorded holder PID is DEAD (kill -0 fails), OR (no PID recorded) older than a defined threshold AND with no live wrapper/git process holding that repo. The reap covers the wrapper’s own lock plus the git-internal existence-based locks (index.lock, HEAD.lock, refs/**/*.lock) that git does NOT auto-release on holder death. It MUST NEVER remove a lock whose holder is ALIVE (removing a live-held lock corrupts a concurrent writer — §9.2). Each reap decision (REAPED / KEPT + reason) is logged as captured evidence (§11.4.6 — liveness PROVEN via kill -0, never assumed). A wrapper that blocks indefinitely on a dead-holder lock (the observed 9-h freeze, 2026-07-04) is a violation. Honest boundary (§11.4.6): reaping clears dead-holder deadlock — it does NOT substitute for the own-.git isolation of §11.4.179 (which shrinks the blast radius from all-streams to one). Classification: universal (§11.4.17). Composes §11.4.84 / §11.4.88 / §11.4.6 / §9.2 / §11.4.116 / §11.4.179. Propagation gate CM-COVENANT-114-180-PROPAGATION (literal 11.4.180) + recommended gate CM-WRAPPER-STALE-LOCK-AUTO-REAP + paired §1.1 mutation. Canonical authority: constitution submodule Constitution.md §11.4.180. Non-compliance is a release blocker. No escape hatch — no --skip-stale-lock-reap, --force-remove-live-lock, --block-on-dead-holder flag.

§11.4.181 — Consistent controlled feature-branch naming: one feature/logic-group ⇒ exactly ONE canonical branch name across the main repo + all owned submodules (User mandate, 2026-07-05). Verbatim (2026-07-05): “System MUST pay attention not to create multiple feat. branches with different names for one same feature or group of workable items / features! Naming MUST BE consistent and carefully controlled!” One feature / logical group of workable items MUST map to EXACTLY ONE canonical branch name, used IDENTICALLY on the main repo AND every touched owned submodule — NEVER two differently-named branches for one feature/group, NEVER per-repo naming drift. ALL hold: (1) single canonical name per group minted ONCE per the §11.4.167 D scheme (feature/<slug> branch + <prefix>-<base>-feat-<slug> tag, <slug> lowercase snake/kebab §11.4.29), used identically across the main repo + every branched submodule (an untouched submodule stays on its base branch, NOT force-branched); (2) single source of truth — the canonical name is recorded ONCE in the §11.4.176 exactly-once claim registry / the §11.4.93 workable-items logic_group → branch mapping and LOOKED UP, never re-invented (creating a branch whose name ≠ the group’s registered canonical name, OR minting a 2nd name for a group that already has one, is a §11.4.6 no-guessing violation); (3) mechanical enforcement (§11.4.75) — gate CM-BRANCH-NAME-CONSISTENCY FAILs on an unregistered feature-branch name / two divergent names for one group / a submodule branch diverging from the group’s main-repo canonical name, paired §1.1 mutation (register one group under two divergent names → gate FAILs); (4) risk-free reconciliation (§9.2 / §11.4.113 / §11.4.84) — a mis-named/duplicate branch is reconciled by MERGING its commits onto the canonical branch (union, no -s ours/rebase/reset that drops commits, git log <canonical>..<misnamed> empty after merge, no conflict markers), ff-only push to all upstreams (NEVER force-push), retiring the mis-named branch only after the merge is confirmed on all mirrors (delete-before-merge forbidden). Honest boundary (§11.4.6): “probably the same feature” is NOT a determination — bind the branch to a group via the registry, never guess. Classification: universal (§11.4.17). Composes §11.4.167 / §11.4.176 / §11.4.178 / §11.4.179 / §11.4.28 / §11.4.29 / §11.4.93 / §11.4.113 / §11.4.84 / §11.4.6 / §11.4.75 / §9.2. Propagation gate CM-COVENANT-114-181-PROPAGATION (literal 11.4.181) + recommended gate CM-BRANCH-NAME-CONSISTENCY + paired §1.1 mutation. Canonical authority: constitution submodule Constitution.md §11.4.181. Non-compliance is a release blocker. No escape hatch — no --allow-divergent-branch-names, --skip-branch-registry, --rename-branch-freely, --per-repo-branch-name-OK, --two-names-one-feature-OK flag.

§11.4.182 — Track+branch work-stream identity label (T<N>/<branch> - <alias>) on every agent/subagent label + every operator-facing work-stream reference (User mandate, 2026-07-05). Operator mandate (2026-07-05): “EVERY agent/subagent/work-stream label AND every operator-facing report reference to a work stream MUST carry a (T<N>/<branch> - <alias>) prefix identifying the track + git branch + the claude-toolkit alias in use — e.g. (T1/main - claude1) ATM-312 MPV drm_prime investigation, (T2/feat/mistiq-vader - deepseek) SPK-XXX ....” In a multi-track layout (parallel checkouts each on a distinct branch and potentially driven by a distinct claude-toolkit alias — e.g. /mnt/track1..4) a bare work-stream label does not say WHICH track/branch/alias produced it, so cross-track dispatches, status reports, and progress lines collide and mislead. ALL hold: (1) universal label form — every agent / subagent / work-stream label AND every operator-facing reference (agent-dispatch descriptions, status reports, resume/handoff docs, progress lines) MUST START with a (T<N>/<branch> - <alias>) prefix; (2) deterministic derivation, never guessed (§11.4.6) — <N> from the checkout path (/mnt/track<N>/…; honest ? when not under a numbered track, never a guessed number), <branch> from git rev-parse --abbrev-ref HEAD, <alias> from the CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR basename .claude-<alias> → <alias> (honest ? when unset/non-matching, never a guessed name) (reference labeler scripts/multitrack/track_branch_label.sh); (3) mechanical enforcement (§11.4.75 / §11.4.109-class) — a PreToolUse guard hook (constitution/scripts/hooks/guard-track-branch-label.sh, inherited BY REFERENCE per §11.4.177, never copied) BLOCKS any Agent/Task/TaskCreate dispatch whose description (or subagent label) does not START with ^\(T[0-9]+/[^ )]+ - [^ )]+\) (branch AND alias both required, both non-empty), non-agent tools untouched; pre-build gate CM-TRACK-BRANCH-LABEL asserts labeler + hook exist/executable/parseable, hook wired in runtime settings, convention doc exists; (4) honest boundary (§11.4.6) — a ? in the track field OR the alias field is a valid honest label (unknown track / no git repo / no matching CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR), never a bluff; the enforced format surfaces (blocks) an off-track dispatch or a dispatch missing its alias segment rather than silently mislabeling it. Classification: universal (§11.4.17). Composes §11.4.178 (the label-prefix instantiation applied to EVERY agent/subagent label + operator-facing reference) / §11.4.29 / §11.4.75 / §11.4.109 / §11.4.177 / §11.4.6 / §11.4.58 / §11.4.103 / §11.4.119 / §11.4.116 / §11.4.147. Propagation gate CM-COVENANT-114-182-PROPAGATION (literal 11.4.182) + recommended gate CM-TRACK-BRANCH-LABEL + paired §1.1 mutation. TRUNK RULE (operator mandate, 2026-07-28): work performed on the canonical trunk (main/master) is ALWAYS Track 1 — the label reads (T1/main - …), NEVER (T?/main - …), regardless of checkout path. Precedence is deliberate: the BRANCH decides first and the checkout path decides only for non-trunk branches, so a trunk checkout living outside /mnt/track<N> — or inside a differently-numbered one — is still Track 1; the identity that matters is which STREAM the work belongs to, not which directory it happens to sit in. Rationale: §11.4.182 labels are how concurrent streams are told apart, so an unnumbered T? on trunk work makes it indistinguishable from a genuinely-unknown track in every dispatch, report and progress line — and the trunk is a well-known, singular stream that is never unknown. Reference implementation: scripts/multitrack/track_branch_label.sh derives the branch BEFORE the track number and maps main|master -> 1. Labels MUST be re-derived from that script at dispatch time and kept in real-time sync with the live branch/alias/model/effort — a remembered or stale label is itself a §11.4.182 violation (§11.4.6: live identity is not knowable from memory). Canonical authority: constitution submodule Constitution.md §11.4.182. Non-compliance is a release blocker. No escape hatch — no --skip-track-label, --unlabeled-agent-OK, --bare-work-stream-reference, --no-track-branch-prefix, --label-from-memory flag.

No escape hatch — no --skip-sonarqube-install, --sonar-cli-optional, --ad-hoc-sonar-path, --rootful-sonar-OK flag. §11.4.185 — Manual QA-team testing as the mandatory FINAL confirmation of done (User mandate, 2026-07-07). Compact summary: no set of features / scope of work / release may be considered fully completed until it has received MANUAL TESTING confirmation by the project’s QA team as the FINAL confirmation step — EVERYWHERE and ALWAYS, never forgotten. Every automated gate (§11.4 anti-bluff, §11.4.5/§11.4.69 captured evidence, §11.4.52 autonomous validation, §11.4.40 full-suite retest, §11.4.108 four-layer verification, §11.4.132 risk-ordered validation) is NECESSARY but NOT SUFFICIENT — manual QA is the FINAL human sufficiency gate. Composition with §11.4.52 ADDS, never weakens: a feature MUST have BOTH the autonomous path (primary, always-on) AND the manual-QA final confirmation — automation-only with no QA sign-off is a §11.4.185 violation; manual-QA-only with no autonomous path is still a §11.4.52 violation. Workflow: autonomous-validation-GREEN → build → flash/deploy → autonomous on-device validation → HAND OFF to the QA team → ONLY after QA-team manual confirmation is the scope “fully completed”; the §11.4.126 autonomous-loop release-tag terminal condition now REQUIRES this confirmation before the tag is cut. Honest boundary (§11.4.6): the agent MUST hand off + WAIT for QA-team confirmation, never self-certify/simulate/infer it; while waiting the agent keeps progressing every OTHER non-blocked item in parallel (§11.4.87/.94/.97/.101) — the wait parks that one scope, not the loop. Classification: universal (§11.4.17). Composes §11.4/.5/.6/.40/.52/.69/.108/.126/.129/.130/.132. Propagation gate CM-COVENANT-114-185-PROPAGATION (literal 11.4.185) + recommended gate CM-MANUAL-QA-FINAL-CONFIRMATION + paired §1.1 mutation. No escape hatch — no --automation-only-is-done, --skip-manual-qa, --self-certify-qa-confirmation, --infer-qa-from-automation flag. §12.12 — Process/thread-limit (RLIMIT_NPROC) awareness for parallel subagent/multi-process work (User mandate, 2026-07-07). Compact summary: heavy parallel subagent/multi-process work is bounded by the OS per-user process/thread limit ulimit -u (RLIMIT_NPROC), which on Linux counts ALL threads AND processes owned by the real UID, not per-process; ambient tooling (MCP servers, language runtimes) can consume most of the budget before project work starts (forensic FACT: a ulimit -u 4096 host sat at ~3900-4005 threads from the ambient MCP fleet alone, leaving under 150 threads of headroom; a 4th Go-heavy subagent hit EAGAIN/failed to create new OS thread). Thread-limit exhaustion is a §12 host-safety event that YIELDS UNCONDITIONALLY, same standing as §12.6 (memory) and §12.7 (AOSP -j cap) — an ORTHOGONAL exhaustion axis, both MUST be checked independently before scaling parallelism. Mandatory pre-scale diagnosis: ulimit -u/ulimit -Hu (soft/hard) + live ps -L --no-headers -u "$USER" | wc -l thread count; headroom = soft minus live; signals (EAGAIN, fork: retry, failed to create new OS thread) never guessed, always captured (§11.4.6); low headroom → SERIALIZE, avoid GOMAXPROCS=nproc-class spikes. Raising the limit — three cited techniques: (a) prlimit --pid <PID> --nproc=<soft>:<hard> (root, no-restart); (b) /etc/security/limits.conf/systemd LimitNPROC (persistent, next-session); (c) self ulimit -u <N> up to the existing hard ceiling (current session) — su -c scope is transient only. Freeing threads by stopping non-essential tooling REQUIRES explicit operator authorization (§11.4.122, no-silent-removal); cmdline-pattern kill helpers MUST exclude the caller’s own $$/$PPID (self-kill hazard). Classification: universal (§11.4.17). Composes §12/.6/.7/§11.4.58/.101/.103/.122/§9.2. Propagation gate CM-COVENANT-12-12-PROPAGATION (literal 12.12) + recommended gate CM-NPROC-HEADROOM-CHECK + paired §1.1 mutation. Canonical authority: constitution submodule Constitution.md §12.12. Non-compliance is a release blocker. No escape hatch — no --ignore-thread-limit, --dispatch-blind, --skip-headroom-check, --autonomous-tooling-kill, --assume-ulimit-scope flag.

§11.4.235 — Build-and-deploy the moment source fixes are proven-correct (test-side hardening is a parallel stage, never a build gate) + post-deploy version increment (operator mandate, 2026-07-27). Cascade reference (short form): (A) the moment a batch’s SOURCE fixes are done AND proven-correct — proven-correct = the mandatory independent SOURCE-correctness review has returned a clean GO (§11.4.125/§11.4.142/§11.4.194 on the source diff, on the §11.4.209 Fable-‘xhigh’ substrate, iterated to zero-finding/zero-warning GO per §11.4.134) — the build MUST be triggered, pre-build + post-build tests EXECUTED (§11.4.108 layer-1 before, layer-2 after), and the artifact deployed; gate-instrumentation hardening + new-gate authoring (§11.4.110), paired §1.1 mutations + four-layer §11.4.4(b) coverage + HelixQA Challenges, ‘gates and checks of all kinds’ (§11.4.96), and the re-review of THAT test-instrumentation work all run IN PARALLEL with the build AND the §11.4.185 manual-QA session, NEVER gating the build (§11.4.230(A)) — the build’s GO-SIGNAL is source-correctness alone, and holding the build sequentially for the test-instrumentation buildout when the source is already proven-correct is a §11.4.235(A) violation. (C) STANDING DEFAULT (§11.4.126/§11.4.230/§11.4.103), never a per-request opt-in. §11.4.236 — QA-deploy-readiness gate: no manual-QA hand-off until the mandated validation produced a candidate-fingerprinted PASS verdict; a blocker MUST bind to a seam, never prose (research-derived, 2026-07-31). Cascade reference (short form): a release artifact MUST NOT be deployed / handed off for manual QA (§11.4.185) until the mandated autonomous validation(s) for THAT artifact produced a machine-written PASS verdict whose artifact fingerprint == the candidate (§11.4.115(F), read FROM THE TARGET at run time); ABSENCE of the verdict BLOCKS the deploy exactly as a FAIL does (§11.4.135) — a not-yet-run validation and a failed one are both refusals, never a silent pass; the refusal lives in the deploy TOOL itself (the seam), never in prose an agent is trusted to honour (§11.4.205/§11.4.226) — a source-gate asserting the validation harness merely EXISTS is necessary-not-sufficient; any requirement an operator declares a blocker (a §11.4.140 CRITICAL, or an explicit “release blocker / QA-ready blocker”) MUST bind to a mechanical seam at the point it gates, never satisfied by prose and never DOWNGRADED to a follow-up, and when the blocker’s own INSTRUMENT bugs the response is §11.4.4/§11.4.120 (STOP, fix the instrument, complete the blocker), never route around it; the seam has NO silent bypass — an override is an explicit, logged, operator-set flag (the §11.4.234/§11.4.135 recorded-deferral pattern); the gate is SELF-VALIDATED (§11.4.107(10)/§11.4.201) with golden-good (verdict present + PASS + fingerprint-match → allow) + golden-bad (absent → refuse / not-pass → refuse / fingerprint-mismatch → refuse) so it cannot itself false-allow, and the verdict-emitter reads the fingerprint from the target at run time, never a hardcoded per-release value (§11.4.111 — a version-pinned validator is the brittleness that lets a stale verdict pass). Honest boundary (§11.4.6): the seam proves a candidate-matching PASS verdict EXISTS for the mandated autonomous validation — it does NOT replace the §11.4.185 manual-QA sufficiency gate (the human check the deploy hands off TO), and it is only as honest as the validation whose verdict it consumes (that validation’s own anti-bluff stays §11.4.5/§11.4.69/§11.4.107). Classification: universal (§11.4.17) — consumers supply the mandated-validation set, the verdict path/schema, and the deploy tool per §11.4.35. §11.4.237 — Mandatory exhaustive context-and-spirit-aware translation review: every localized artifact independently reviewed for CONTEXT (source) + SPIRIT (target-language idiom/register) + technical/non-translatable fidelity — accuracy necessary, never sufficient (operator mandate, 2026-08-05). Cascade reference (short form): every translation and every translated artifact — UI strings, pages, documents, PDFs, manuals, marketing copy, captions/subtitles, help text, any localized content — MUST undergo a HEAVY, EXHAUSTIVE, INDEPENDENT review by a reviewer (agent or human) STRUCTURALLY SEPARATE from the translator that produced it (§11.4.70/§11.4.20 independence; the translator’s own §11.4.92 multi-pass self-review PRECEDES it and NEVER satisfies it, per §11.4.142’s no-self-review-substitution discipline applied to localization) — a localized artifact accepted without an independent context-and-spirit review is a §11.4 PASS-bluff at the localization layer. The reviewer holds and applies THREE dimensions: (a) FULL CONTEXT OF THE SOURCE MATERIAL — the original ideas, the subject/domain, the purpose, the intended audience; the reviewer reads the source MEANING, not merely the source STRING, so intent, nuance and tone are carried across, never lost; (b) THE SPIRIT OF THE TARGET LANGUAGE — its tradition, idiom, register, tone and cultural conventions — so the result reads NATIVE, natural, fluent and MAXIMALLY POLISHED, never literal / word-for-word / calqued, a construction correct in the source that reads foreign in the target being REWRITTEN into the natural target-language form; (c) CORRECT TECHNICAL VOCABULARY AND VERBATIM PRESERVATION OF NON-TRANSLATABLE TERMS — domain/technical terms rendered with their established target-language equivalents, and non-translatable tokens (product names, brand names, proper nouns, technology/library/API names, code identifiers, trademarks) preserved VERBATIM, never “translated”, never accidentally transliterated, never localized away. The goal is a slick, polished, professional, native-reading localization: fidelity to the source is the FLOOR (accuracy NECESSARY), never the ceiling (accuracy NEVER SUFFICIENT) — a rendering that is accurate but stilted / foreign / register-wrong / idiom-broken is a FINDING, not a pass. Anti-bluff: the review emits captured, machine-readable POSITIVE evidence per §11.4.5/§11.4.69 (feature_class = translation_review) — a per-artifact verdict carrying a PER-DIMENSION rationale across the closed dimension set {fidelity, fluency/naturalness, spirit/register, terminology, completeness, non-translatable-preservation}; a PASS with no captured per-dimension rationale is a §11.4/§11.4.1 bluff, and a FAIL on ANY dimension LOOPS BACK to re-translation/polish and re-review, iterating to a zero-finding clean verdict per §11.4.134 (a single pass that “looked fine” never closes the artifact). Honest boundary (§11.4.6): this anchor REFINES the existing localization discipline from PASS/FAIL accuracy checking to CONTEXT-AND-SPIRIT native-polish review — it does NOT replace §11.4.140’s canonical translation pipeline nor §11.4.141’s independent per-language review (a KNOWN operator-owned §11.4.54 anchor-number collision on 11.4.140/11.4.141, cited here strictly in those senses, re-mint still operator-owned, introducing no new collision), it STRENGTHENS them (accuracy → native polish); it does not make a mistranslation impossible (a miscalibrated reviewer stays §11.4.107(10) golden-good/golden-bad fixture territory), and where a target language genuinely has no established term for a source concept that gap is an honestly recorded finding (§11.4.3 SKIP-with-reason), never a silently invented word. Classification: universal (§11.4.17) — no vendor/project/language literal; consumers supply their language set, term glossary, non-translatable token list, and reviewer substrate as DATA per §11.4.35. Composes §11.4.140/.141/.6/.69/.92/.134/.142/.209/§1.1. Propagation gate CM-COVENANT-114-237-PROPAGATION (literal 11.4.237) + recommended gate CM-TRANSLATION-CONTEXT-SPIRIT-REVIEW + paired §1.1 mutation (gate-code = separate work item, NOT claimed shipped §11.4.6/§11.4.227). Canonical authority: constitution submodule Constitution.md §11.4.237 — the full mandate text is canonical there and is inherited by reference (CONST-059 / §11.4.35). Non-compliance is a release blocker. No escape hatch — no --skip-spirit-review, --accuracy-only-OK, --self-review-translation-OK, --literal-translation-OK, --translate-non-translatable-OK, --skip-per-dimension-rationale flag.

§11.4.238 — Automated QA must be the DISCOVERER: manual QA finds nothing new, and anything it finds is a coverage escape (User mandate, 2026-08-08). Verbatim operator mandate: “Helix QA full websites testing MUST find all such issues, not us! Once we do manual QA we shall not be able to find anything! THIS IS MANDATORY RULE / CONSTRAIN / GOAL to achieve with this and any other project and MUST BE part of the root constitution!” Forensic case study (FACT, 2026-08-08): in one cycle five real defects were found — a health endpoint returning {"status":"healthy","components":[]} that was structurally incapable of ever reporting unhealthy; a gateway routing every completion at a port nothing served (HTTP 500 on the product’s primary capability); two submodule pins on main that upstream answers with not our ref, so no fresh clone could check them out; and two hardware-profiler defects reading a key from a file where it does not exist. Every one was found by an agent reading source or probing by hand; not one by the automated QA system, which reported green throughout. The mandate (ALL hold): (A) the automated QA system is the DISCOVERER, not the confirmer — discovery by a human, an operator’s manual pass, an agent reading code, or an end-user report is a FAILURE of the QA system; the finding is still fixed, but the QA gap it exposes is a defect of equal standing and closing only the reported defect is the violation. (B) manual QA must find NOTHING NEW — by the time manual QA runs (§11.4.185) the automated regime must already have surfaced everything, so manual QA is a CONFIRMATION and sufficiency gate, never the discovery mechanism; the target is explicit and measurable — a manual pass discovering zero previously-unknown defects. (C) every escape triggers the §11.4.138 loop, widened to EVERY out-of-band channel: systematic-debug the defect (§11.4.102), produce a coverage-escape audit naming WHY the regime missed it cited to the specific missing check / unexercised path / absent assertion (never “we didn’t think of it”), register a new or strengthened automated check that WOULD have caught it into the standing regime (§11.4.135) with a §11.4.115 RED capturing the escaped defect, and commit the audit as captured evidence. (D) coverage is measured by what the regime can CATCH, not what it runs — every surface falsifiable per §1.1 (a check whose paired mutation does not make it fail is decoration), exercising real user-reachable paths end-to-end (§11.4.98 / §11.4.143 / §11.4.136 / §11.4.158 / §11.4.159 / §11.4.160). (E) directional and measured — track the discovery-channel split (automated vs out-of-band) and drive out-of-band toward zero; honest boundary (§11.4.6): this does NOT claim a regime can be provably complete (§11.4.118) and does NOT license closing an escape by narrowing the definition of “defect”. Composes §11.4.27 / §11.4.138 (trigger set widened) / §11.4.185 (manual QA becomes confirmation, never discovery) / §11.4.118 / §11.4.135 / §11.4.115 / §11.4.98 / §11.4.136 / §11.4.143 / §11.4.158 / §11.4.159 / §11.4.160 / §11.4.102 / §11.4.6 / §1.1. Classification: universal (§11.4.17) — the consuming project supplies its QA system, surface inventory, and discovery-channel ledger per §11.4.35. Propagation gate CM-COVENANT-114-238-PROPAGATION (literal 11.4.238) + recommended gates CM-QA-IS-THE-DISCOVERER / CM-MANUAL-QA-FINDS-NOTHING-NEW + paired §1.1 mutation (gate-code = separate work item, NOT claimed shipped §11.4.6/§11.4.227).

Canonical authority: constitution submodule Constitution.md §11.4.238. Non-compliance is a release blocker. No escape hatch — no --manual-qa-may-discover, --skip-coverage-escape-audit, --found-by-human-is-fine, --close-escape-without-new-check, --qa-green-is-enough, --discovery-channel-not-tracked flag.

CONST-051(A) — Project instantiation: the concrete equal-codebase roster (§11.4.35)

Operator mandate (2026-09-02): “Make sure that all of our systems, sub-systems, submodules and everything we control in our organizations repos is treated equally as part of the project — work done on them all equally as on main repo module itself! Especially Helix Agent, LLM, Translate, QA, etc. Make sure that this fact is part of the local project’s constitution so it is absolutely clear.”

The universal rule already exists: CONST-051(A) / §11.4.28(A) equal-codebase. This section is its §11.4.35 project instantiation — the concrete roster the universal rule requires the consuming project to supply, so “every owned submodule” is never an abstraction anyone has to guess at.

The rule, stated without hedging

Every repository listed below is an equal part of HelixCode’s codebase — not a dependency, not a vendored blob, not somebody else’s problem. Work performed on any of them carries exactly the same obligations as work on the main module:

  • the same anti-bluff posture — every PASS carries captured runtime evidence (§11.4, §11.4.5, §11.4.123)
  • the same test-type floor (§11.4.169) and four-layer coverage (§11.4.4(b))
  • the same TDD discipline and coverage floor (§11.4.224)
  • the same independent code review, iterated to a zero-finding GO (§11.4.125 / §11.4.134 / §11.4.142)
  • the same documentation, user manuals and guides, kept in sync (§11.4.12 / §11.4.65)
  • the same Challenges + HelixQA coverage (CONST-050(B))
  • the same gates and paired §1.1 mutations
  • the same release discipline — prefixed tags (§11.4.151), ff-only pushes, never force (§11.4.113)

A round of work that improves the main module while leaving an owned submodule’s equivalent deficiency unaddressed is a CONST-051(A) violation, regardless of how good the main-module work was. Coverage ledgers (§11.4.48) list every repository below as in-scope.

The roster — 118 owned repositories of 131 total (measured 2026-09-02)

HelixDevelopment (13) — the Helix family the operator named explicitly:

grep -E '^\s*(path|url)' .gitmodules | paste - - | grep -E 'vasic-digital|HelixDevelopment'

Not on the roster — 13 third-party submodules are READ-ONLY. Any repository whose URL owner is neither vasic-digital nor HelixDevelopment* (llama.cpp, ollama, huggingface_hub, docling, skyvern, mcp servers, the cli_agents_resources/* reference corpora, …) is vendored: we consume it, we do not modify it, and it is pinned at an upstream revision. Local modifications to these are drift to be reverted, never work to be committed.

Honest boundary (§11.4.6)

The operator’s mandate named “Helix Agent, LLM, Translate, QA”. Agent, LLM and QA are on the roster above and verified present. There is no helix_translate submodule in this project — grep helix_translate .gitmodules returns 0 matches, and the only occurrence of that name anywhere in the governance corpus is as an illustrative example string inside the §11.4.151 release-prefix rule (helix_translate-1.0.0-dev-0.0.1). It is recorded here as NOT INCORPORATED rather than silently listed as though it existed. If Helix Translate is a real repository that belongs in this project, adding it is a §11.4.74 catalogue-check + submodule-incorporation task, and this roster must be updated in the same commit.

Roster maintenance: this list is data, and it drifts. Any commit that adds or removes an owned submodule MUST update this section in the same commit — a stale roster under-states the equal-codebase surface and is itself a CONST-051(A) defect.

§11.4.239 — Critical-invariant work-class Definition of Done mandate — money/safety/availability/integrity code MUST include failure-path scenarios as gates, not merely happy-path passes (research-derived, 2026-08-15). Cascade reference (short form): every project’s Definition of Done (§11.4.15/§11.4.16 lifecycle + §11.4.33 closure vocabulary) MUST identify a closed CRITICAL-INVARIANT WORK CLASS — code paths where a single incorrect outcome is unrecoverable in the window between failure and remediation — and MUST require that a change touching that class carry EXPLICIT FAILURE-PATH SCENARIOS turned into passing tests BEFORE closure; a DoD stopping at “code compiles + happy-path test green” on such code is “a machine for generating reopened tickets on the paths that move money”. ALL hold: (A) four universal categories, consumer-extended as DATA per §11.4.35 — MONEY (credit/debit/transfer/hold/release/refund/reconcile), SAFETY (outputs actuating hardware / medical / transport / industrial controls), AVAILABILITY (the primary user-facing capability’s critical path), INTEGRITY (mutations of a persistent record whose corruption cannot be silently repaired); a region is IN the class when at least one category applies, and the four are NOT claimed exhaustive for every domain. (B) failure paths are FIRST-CLASS acceptance criteria enumerated BEFORE the code is authored, each a Given/When/Then scenario whose “Then” observes a real user-observable outcome (a ledger row, a settled charge, a state delta) rather than “no error” (§11.4.69); the reference MONEY minimum is FRESH / IDEMPOTENT RETRY / SAME-KEY-DIFFERENT-PAYLOAD / CRASH-IN-THE-MIDDLE (canon: “the two criteria juniors omit are same-key-different-payload … and the crash-in-the-middle”), with analogous consumer-supplied minima for SAFETY, AVAILABILITY and INTEGRITY; an omitted scenario is a finding of §11.4/§11.4.1 severity, never a “we’ll add it if it bites us” deferral. (C) the precondition binds MECHANICALLY through the existing §11.4.146(D3) status-custody chain — a done/ready/closed-status write against an item whose diff touches the class is REFUSED unless the registry row cites coverage of the declared scenario set, each scenario mapped to a §11.4.115(F) machine-written verdict pair carrying §11.4.226 class-matched evidence; an “N/A” scenario MUST cite a §11.4.6-honest reason, a bare “N/A” is treated as omission. (D) composes §11.4.108 (scenarios close on runtime-class evidence, never source-green), §11.4.129 (huge-blocker protocol governs a scenario-surfaced defect during release validation), §11.4.185 (manual QA un-weakened — automation NECESSARY never sufficient) and §11.4.238 (a manual-QA-discovered failure-path defect is BOTH a coverage-escape audit and an acceptance-criteria audit). (E) honest boundary (§11.4.6) — DIRECTIONAL: it forbids the “happy path suffices” shape and mandates a domain-appropriate minimum, it does NOT claim exhaustive enumeration of every failure mode (§11.4.118 discovery-pressure territory); each escape becomes a permanent addition to the scenario set (§11.4.135). Classification: universal (§11.4.17). Propagation gate CM-COVENANT-114-239-PROPAGATION (literal 11.4.239) + recommended gate CM-CRITICAL-INVARIANT-FAILURE-PATH-DOD + paired §1.1 mutation (gate-code = separate work item, NOT claimed shipped §11.4.6/§11.4.227). Canonical authority: constitution submodule Constitution.md §11.4.239 — the full mandate text is canonical there and is inherited by reference (CONST-059 / §11.4.35). Non-compliance is a release blocker. No escape hatch — no --happy-path-suffices-for-money, --skip-failure-scenarios, --defer-failure-path-tests, --criticality-not-applicable-without-reason, --n/a-without-justification, --closure-without-scenario-map flag.

§11.4.240 — Producer ≠ Verifier scope separation: the actor that produces a change/artifact/verdict cannot be the one that gates it — least privilege over cleverness (research-derived, 2026-08-15). Cascade reference (short form): every project MUST enforce STRUCTURAL SEPARATION between the actor that PRODUCES a change/artifact/verdict and the actor that GATES it — the producer cannot write, weaken, delete or edit the acceptance tests, the oracle, the gate configuration, the verdict store, the paired §1.1 mutation, or any other check whose green result certifies the producer’s own work. ALL hold: (A) FOUR SCOPES that MUST resolve to distinct actors — PRODUCER (writes the diff/config/migration/artifact code), ORACLE (decides “correct output” per §11.4.245), GATE (decides pass/fail — CI config, gate scripts, §11.4.146(D3) custody enforcer, §11.4.135 release-verdict machinery), VERIFIER (re-runs the gate from a CLEAN CHECKOUT and attaches receipts per §11.4.165/§11.4.209/§11.4.194); canon: “the agent that wrote the code cannot be the authority that certifies it — self-preference bias and shared context make self-review worthless … Remove any one property and it degrades to self-certification”; a four-scope collapse is maximum-severity, any two-scope collapse involving the producer is a finding of §11.4/§11.4.1 severity. (B) enforcement is by CAPABILITY, not INSTRUCTION — “least privilege over cleverness … if it can’t touch the gate, it can’t game it”: filesystem scope excluding tests//oracle//CI-config/gate/verdict paths, branch + review protection (§11.4.248 PROTECTED-SPEC + CODEOWNERS), verdict-store isolation (§11.4.115(F) harness writes, never the producer’s code path), and execution isolation (fresh context, clean checkout, no inherited dirty worktree); “we told it in the prompt not to hardcode” is canon’s named shallow/red-flag answer — exhortation is refused as the enforcement layer. (C) THREE FORBIDDEN COLLAPSES — PRODUCER=ORACLE (tautological testing; countered by authoring the oracle from spec / §11.4.243 characterization baseline / §11.4.245 derived reference), PRODUCER=GATE (silenced gates / weakened thresholds; countered by §11.4.248-protected paths + a non-producer approver), PRODUCER=VERIFIER (self-certification; countered by §11.4.115(F) machine-written fingerprint-keyed verdicts + the §11.4.135 release seam refusing producer-asserted verdicts). (D) composes the existing verifier layer (§11.4.115(F)/§11.4.125/§11.4.135/§11.4.142/§11.4.146(D3)/§11.4.165/§11.4.194/§11.4.209) — §11.4.240 ARTICULATES the property those mechanisms enforce and forbids the collapses that would make them decoration. (E) honest boundary (§11.4.6) — separation forbids the CLASS of self-certification; it does NOT prove the verifier correct (§11.4.107(10)), nor the oracle correct (§11.4.245), nor close every gaming vector (over-fitting to seen cases stays §11.4.245 held-out/property/metamorphic territory). (F) INDEPENDENCE-TIER VOCABULARY + TIER-C TRIGGERS, DEGRADING HONESTLY (extension, 2026-08-26) — every accepted verdict records its achieved basis of independence from the CLOSED SET {instance, model, capability}; model diversity is PERMITTED, NEVER REQUIRED (refusing a same-model independent verdict at an ordinary seam is itself the §11.4.201 false-positive-refusal class), and this does NOT weaken §11.4.256’s different-model translation-review pin nor §11.4.209’s Fable-xhigh code-review pin; Tier-C strength is triggered by (1) the producer holding write access to the evidence/verdict store, or (2) the release-tag / qa-deploy / manual-qa-handoff seams (where a producer-authored verdict is REFUSED regardless); outside those two triggers a separate agent INSTANCE MUST SUFFICE and capability separation MUST NOT be demanded; on a SINGLE-UID host capability separation cannot be established by any mechanism an agent can set up, so such a host reports instance HONESTLY and never capability — the field records what was ACHIEVED, never what was ASPIRED to, and honest degradation IS the compliant path. Classification: universal (§11.4.17). Propagation gate CM-COVENANT-114-240-PROPAGATION (literal 11.4.240) + recommended gates CM-PRODUCER-NOT-VERIFIER-SCOPE-SPLIT / CM-LEAST-PRIVILEGE-CAPABILITY-NOT-INSTRUCTION / CM-INDEPENDENCE-TIER-HONESTLY-RECORDED + paired §1.1 mutations (gate-code = separate work item, NOT claimed shipped §11.4.6/§11.4.227). Canonical authority: constitution submodule Constitution.md §11.4.240 — the full mandate text is canonical there and is inherited by reference (CONST-059 / §11.4.35). Non-compliance is a release blocker. No escape hatch — no --producer-may-write-tests, --self-certification-OK, --exhortation-suffices-as-enforcement, --producer-authored-verdict-accepted, --capability-limit-optional, --policy-document-is-enforcement, --single-actor-for-all-scopes flag.

§11.4.241 — Illegal-state-unrepresentability preference: types before API-shape before lint before property-test before runtime-assertion (research-derived, 2026-08-15). Cascade reference (short form): every load-bearing invariant a change relies on MUST land at the STRONGEST enforcement rung the target language/toolchain supports, on the closed ordered ladder (1) TYPE SYSTEM → (2) API SHAPE → (3) LINT → (4) PROPERTY-BASED TEST → (5) RUNTIME ASSERTION; a defect PREVENTABLE at rung N but caught only at rung > N is a finding. Rung-by-language capability baseline: strongly-typed languages reach rungs 1–5; dynamically-typed with gradual typing reach rung 1 conditionally; untyped scripting reaches rungs 3–5 unconditionally. Genuinely-limited-environment clarification: a POSIX-sh surface with only rung 5 SATISFIES the anchor IFF the runtime assertion GENUINELY CATCHES the invariant AND is paired with its §1.1 meta-test mutation per §11.4.107(10) self-validation. Composes §11.4.108 (pushes enforcement LEFT of Layer 1) / §11.4.115 / §11.4.142 / §11.4.194 / §11.4.201 / §11.4.224 / §11.4.6 / §11.4.35 / §1.1. Classification: universal (§11.4.17). Propagation gate CM-COVENANT-114-241-PROPAGATION (literal 11.4.241) + recommended gate CM-ILLEGAL-STATE-LADDER-PREFERENCE + paired §1.1 mutation (gate-code = separate work item, NOT claimed shipped §11.4.6/§11.4.227). Canonical authority: constitution submodule Constitution.md §11.4.241 — the full mandate text is canonical there and is inherited by reference (CONST-059 / §11.4.35); the canonical block is pointer-plus-summary form naming a curriculum_anchors_241_254_full_drafts.md draft NOT present in this checkout, so this reference derives strictly from the in-canon summary (§11.4.6). Non-compliance is a release blocker. No escape hatch — no --runtime-assert-suffices, --skip-type-preference, --dynamic-language-exempts-all-rungs, --property-test-in-lieu-of-type, --decorative-type-annotation-OK flag.

§11.4.242 — Bisection-not-blame regression cause-finding + failure modes (research-derived, 2026-08-15). Cascade reference (short form): when a previously-working feature/behaviour regresses, the FIRST-choice localisation mechanism is BISECTION (git bisect or an equivalent monotone search, O(log N)) over the ordered commit range between the last known-good tag (§11.4.114) and current HEAD — NOT blame-log inspection, NOT speculative root-cause hunting. MONOTONE-SEARCH INVARIANT: bisection is valid only when the observable is monotone across the range (broken stays broken); a non-monotone observable (flaky, environment-dependent, timing-race) MUST be stabilised first, because bisection over a flaky signal produces a WRONG “guilty” commit with the same confidence as a correct one (a §11.4/§11.4.1 bluff at the localisation layer). CLOSED 6-CLASS FAILURE-MODE CATALOGUE: (1) flaky/non-monotone observable; (2) build-broken commits mistaken for “bad” (use bisect skip, exit 125); (3) merge-commit introductions (the defect is in the conflict resolution, in neither parent); (4) external/environmental changes; (5) delayed-fuse introductions (latent commit + later trigger); (6) test-infra regressions masquerading as product regressions. A git bisect run script MUST honour exit-code discipline (0=good, 1–124=bad, 125=skip, 128+=abort) — mapping every non-zero to “bad” collapses the taxonomy. The bisection RESULT is captured evidence (§11.4.5/§11.4.69) feeding §11.4.102’s root-cause phase. EXTENSION — DELTA DEBUGGING (2026-08-20): git bisect IS delta debugging over commits, not the reverse; when a failure is not a monotone commit-range regression, or when a commit-bisection returns a change too large to explain it, the investigation MUST narrow along the discriminating axis — input, config, data, dependency or feature flag — to a MINIMAL failing case that becomes the §11.4.115 RED test’s input, under “a stable, fast, unambiguous test predicate”, running the predicate multiple times per step where the failure is noisy (§11.4.50/§11.4.248); minimisation yields the smallest input that still triggers the failure, which canon says “usually is the diagnosis”, NOT the root cause (that stays §11.4.102). Honest boundary (§11.4.6): bisection localises the INTRODUCING COMMIT of a MONOTONE regression; it does NOT prove that commit’s change is the ROOT CAUSE. Classification: universal (§11.4.17). Propagation gate CM-COVENANT-114-242-PROPAGATION (literal 11.4.242) + recommended gates CM-BISECTION-BEFORE-BLAME / CM-DELTA-DEBUG-MINIMAL-CASE + paired §1.1 mutations (gate-code = separate work item, NOT claimed shipped §11.4.6/§11.4.227). Canonical authority: constitution submodule Constitution.md §11.4.242 — the full mandate text is canonical there and is inherited by reference (CONST-059 / §11.4.35). Non-compliance is a release blocker. No escape hatch — no --blame-log-suffices, --skip-bisection, --flaky-observable-bisect-anyway, --run-script-exit-code-collapse-OK, --regression-guess-without-localisation flag.

§11.4.243 — Characterization / golden-master safety net for legacy code before behaviour-changing modification (research-derived, 2026-08-15). Cascade reference (short form): before ANY behaviour-changing modification to legacy code lacking characterization tests, the modifier MUST FIRST author CHARACTERIZATION TESTS asserting the CURRENT observable behaviour as GROUND TRUTH (whether “correct” or not) and land them as a green baseline; only AFTER the safety net exists may the modification proceed. The pattern originates in Feathers’s WORKING EFFECTIVELY WITH LEGACY CODE and generalises GOLDEN-MASTER (record outputs across a representative input space; assert future runs match). LEGACY SCOPE (§11.4.6-honest, any one suffices): (i) lacks tests asserting observable behaviour; (ii) written spec diverges from measured actual behaviour; (iii) predates the constitution’s TDD discipline (§11.4.224); (iv) the modifier cannot describe its exact behaviour from reading source within the time budget. AUTHORING LOOP: identify the observable surface → generate a representative input space → run and record actual outputs as the ground-truth baseline → assert the recorded values → commit the characterization tests as a SEPARATE commit BEFORE the behaviour-changing modification. DISTINCTION FROM CORRECTNESS: characterization is DESCRIPTIVE not prescriptive — it captures WHAT IS, not WHAT SHOULD BE; where current behaviour is subtly wrong the test CAPTURES that wrongness and the modifier MUST notice and ASK the operator per §11.4.66 whether to preserve or correct it, never decide silently. Composes §11.4.115 (RED-first — characterization is its legacy counterpart) / §11.4.124 (investigate-before-remove, the parallel discipline) / §11.4.118 (a sampled input space is an honest gap, not completeness) / §11.4.201 / §11.4.224. Classification: universal (§11.4.17). §11.4.244 — Cross-boundary contract tests + can-i-deploy gate (research-derived, 2026-08-15). Cascade reference (short form): a change modifying a boundary between independently-deployable components (service↔︎consumer, library↔︎dependents, DB schema↔︎readers/writers, message topic↔︎subscribers) MUST be covered by CONSUMER-DRIVEN CONTRACT TESTS on BOTH sides, AND a can-i-deploy gate MUST verify the proposed provider version’s compatibility against every currently-deployed consumer version BEFORE the change ships. §11.4.244 deliberately MERGES the two — they are useless in isolation: contract tests without the gate produce compatibility knowledge nobody consults, and a can-i-deploy gate without contract tests has nothing to check. CDC pattern: consumers publish the assumptions they make about the provider (Pact / Spring Cloud Contract / gRPC schema-evolution rules); providers verify each consumer’s contract against the actual implementation; a provider change breaking a live consumer’s contract FAILS THE PROVIDER’S BUILD before the change ships — “a precise red at the source”. CAN-I-DEPLOY: at every deploy attempt the gate queries the contract broker for the compatibility matrix; a single red cell BLOCKS the deploy with a machine-readable verdict naming the incompatible pair, NEVER warning-with-continue; a MISSING contract is a first-class refusal reason per §11.4.201 (a false-positive REFUSAL is a FAIL-bluff exactly as a false-negative allowance is a PASS-bluff). SemVer becomes MEASURABLE — a PATCH bump that breaks a contract is a mis-labelled version. The backward-compatibility window is declared per §11.4.35. Composes §11.4.5 / §11.4.6 / §11.4.35 / §11.4.66 / §11.4.108 (per-side four-layer) / §11.4.135 (contract tests as permanent regression guards) / §11.4.185 (composed-system manual QA still needed) / §11.4.201 / §1.1. Classification: universal (§11.4.17). Propagation gate CM-COVENANT-114-244-PROPAGATION (literal 11.4.244) + recommended gates CM-CONTRACT-TESTS-BOTH-SIDES / CM-CAN-I-DEPLOY-GATE-WIRED + paired §1.1 mutations (gate-code = separate work item, NOT claimed shipped §11.4.6/§11.4.227). Canonical authority: constitution submodule Constitution.md §11.4.244 — the full mandate text is canonical there and is inherited by reference (CONST-059 / §11.4.35). Non-compliance is a release blocker. No escape hatch — no --skip-contract-tests, --deploy-without-compatibility-check, --assume-compatible-when-broker-silent, --patch-may-break-contract, --contract-tests-optional-for-internal-boundaries flag.

§11.4.245 — Oracle-problem-first test authoring: identify the oracle BEFORE authoring the test (research-derived, 2026-08-15). Cascade reference (short form): every test MUST FIRST identify its ORACLE — the mechanism deciding PASS vs FAIL for the input it drives — and that oracle MUST be DIFFERENT from the code under test AND from the test’s own assertion mechanism; authoring without explicit oracle identification produces the “test agrees with the code” anti-pattern (the test asserts what the code happens to produce, so it passes as long as the code produces something and never catches wrong-output — the most common form of §11.4/§11.4.1 bluff at the test-authoring layer). §11.4.245 does not SOLVE the oracle problem (the oldest unsolved problem in software testing); it mandates HONESTLY IDENTIFYING which oracle strategy each test uses so the §11.4.194 reviewer can judge whether that oracle is genuinely independent. CLOSED ORACLE-STRATEGY SET, named explicitly in each test’s source — (1) SPECIFIED (a spec/RFC/standard names the expected output); (2) DERIVED (an independent reference implementation); (3) METAMORPHIC (relations between related inputs — §11.4.107(8) generalised); (4) GOLDEN-MASTER / CHARACTERIZATION (past captured behaviour — §11.4.243); (5) INVARIANT (a universal property that must hold — sort output sorted AND a permutation of the input); (6) STATISTICAL (distribution properties for stochastic outputs, never a specific value); (7) HUMAN (§11.4.185 manual QA). NO other classes. STRUCTURAL INDEPENDENCE: the oracle’s SOURCE of the expected value must not depend on the code being tested. §11.4.107(10) SELF-VALIDATION generalised: the paired §1.1 mutation must produce a WRONG output and the oracle must catch it. Composes §11.4.5 / §11.4.6 / §11.4.107(10) / §11.4.115 / §11.4.194 / §11.4.201 / §11.4.224 / §11.4.243 / §1.1. Classification: universal (§11.4.17). Propagation gate CM-COVENANT-114-245-PROPAGATION (literal 11.4.245) + recommended gate CM-ORACLE-STRATEGY-NAMED-AND-INDEPENDENT + paired §1.1 mutation (gate-code = separate work item, NOT claimed shipped §11.4.6/§11.4.227). Canonical authority: constitution submodule Constitution.md §11.4.245 — the full mandate text is canonical there and is inherited by reference (CONST-059 / §11.4.35). Non-compliance is a release blocker. No escape hatch — no --test-agrees-with-code, --skip-oracle-identification, --oracle-is-the-code, --assertion-is-oracle, --implicit-oracle-OK flag.

§11.4.246 — Reproducible + hermetic builds + supply-chain integrity at SLSA Build Level 2 (operator decision, 2026-08-15). Cascade reference (short form): every consuming project’s build pipeline MUST produce REPRODUCIBLE, HERMETIC artifacts and MUST meet SLSA Build Level 2 as the FLEET-WIDE MINIMUM (operator decision 2026-08-15; Level 3+ tracked as backlog per §11.4.197). REPRODUCIBLE = the same source at the same time on any conformant host produces byte-identical artifacts, or a diff-set fully accounted for by declared non-determinisms pinned to canonical values. HERMETIC = no undeclared inputs — every dependency version-pinned and lockfile-hashed, every tool version declared, no unpinned public-network reach during the build, no host-environment leakage. SLSA-L2 = signed provenance describing source URI + commit hash + builder identity + invocation + artifact hash, produced on a hosted tamper-resistant build platform (the project’s ancillary posture is already satisfied by SSH-only remotes §2.1, rootless podman §11.4.161, fixed-tag pins §11.4.30/§11.4.77). Every consumer MAINTAINS docs/security/SLSA_LEVEL.md (or its §11.4.35 equivalent) recording the CURRENT level with cited evidence, the TARGET level, and tracked §11.4.197 upgrade items — a CURRENT below L2 with no tracked item is a violation. SUPPLY-CHAIN EXTENSION: dependencies are either vendored (checked in, hash-verified), sourced from a provenance-attested registry, OR mirrored through a hash-pinning local registry. THREAT MODEL IS NARROW: it covers the source→artifact chain; it does NOT cover a malicious commit landing in source (§11.4.142 review), a compromised dependency source, or a bug-free artifact (§11.4.108 four-layer + §11.4.185 manual QA). Composes §2.1 / §11.4.6 / §11.4.30 / §11.4.35 / §11.4.108 / §11.4.113 / §11.4.135 / §11.4.142 / §11.4.161 / §11.4.184 / §11.4.185 / §11.4.197 / §11.4.209 / §1.1. Classification: universal (§11.4.17). Propagation gate CM-COVENANT-114-246-PROPAGATION (literal 11.4.246) + recommended gates CM-BUILD-REPRODUCIBILITY / CM-BUILD-HERMETICITY / CM-SLSA-L2-PROVENANCE-VERIFIED / CM-SUPPLY-CHAIN-DEPS-PROVENANCE-ATTESTED + paired §1.1 mutations (gate-code = separate work item, NOT claimed shipped §11.4.6/§11.4.227). Canonical authority: constitution submodule Constitution.md §11.4.246 — the full mandate text is canonical there and is inherited by reference (CONST-059 / §11.4.35). §11.4.247 — Composite-output layer-move must move every layer (research-derived, 2026-08-15). Cascade reference (short form): a change MOVING a system-boundary layer (a service migrating hosts, an endpoint URL change, an API version bump, a DB schema shift, a message-topic rename, an ingress path re-route, a storage-bucket move) MUST move EVERY LAYER of the composite output atomically or via an explicit compatibility window — NEVER piecemeal. LAYER TAXONOMY (§11.4.35 baseline, consumer-supplied concrete inventory): (i) server (handler + route + OpenAPI/proto/gRPC descriptor); (ii) client (call-sites + SDK stubs + integration tests); (iii) infrastructure (load balancer + ingress + API gateway + CDN + service mesh + DNS); (iv) data stores (cached keys + DB URL rows + MQ subscriptions); (v) observability (metric labels + span names + log fields + dashboards + alerts + SLOs); (vi) documentation (user + dev + runbook + README + cookbook); (vii) tests (integration + e2e + contract per §11.4.244 + load + smoke). The consuming project provides a scripts/audit/layer_move_completeness.sh-class residual-reference report; a change proposing a move MUST show ZERO residuals OR an explicit compatibility-window plan per residual with tracked §11.4.197 items. COMPATIBILITY WINDOW: the old value keeps working alongside the new until a stated date/version/event, deprecation warnings are named per caller, the deadline is tracked, and removal at expiry is clean — never “clean up later”. Canon records a MATERIAL-THIN + CITATION-AUDIT note: the phrases “composite-output”, “surface-swap-not-recreate” and “atomic move discipline” have ZERO occurrences in the tracked corpus and are this anchor’s OWN PARAPHRASE, not quoted source text; the module-32 DOMAIN attribution is plausible and unrefuted while the PHRASE-level attribution is UNVERIFIED (§11.4.6). Composes §11.4.6 / §11.4.35 / §11.4.111 / §11.4.118 / §11.4.124 / §11.4.135 / §11.4.191 / §11.4.197 / §11.4.238 (a residual IS a coverage escape) / §11.4.244 / §1.1. Classification: universal (§11.4.17). Canonical authority: constitution submodule Constitution.md §11.4.247 — the full mandate text is canonical there and is inherited by reference (CONST-059 / §11.4.35). §11.4.248 — Flaky-test quarantine + protected-regression-spec gate via [PROTECTED-SPEC: ATM-NNN] tag + CODEOWNERS required reviewer (operator decision, 2026-08-15). Cascade reference (short form): flaky tests are the SILENT ANTI-BLUFF DECAY vector — every flake conditions the team to ignore red until a real regression is dismissed as “probably flaky, re-run”. Two disciplines bind: (A) QUARANTINE — mechanical detection (a test-history aggregator flagging a two-consecutive-run-different-verdict or a rerun-after-red pattern) moves the flaky test to tests/quarantine/ (path per §11.4.35), opens a tracked §11.4.197 stabilisation deadline, and the blocking suite runs WITHOUT the quarantined test so green means green; a deadline past due is a §11.4.66 extension or a violation. (B) PROTECTED-REGRESSION-SPEC — a regression test codifying a known-broken defect fix (per the §11.4.135 permanent-guard suite) MAY be tagged # [PROTECTED-SPEC: ATM-NNN] (or the target language’s comment equivalent) referencing the §11.4.54 workable item it guards, marking it a load-bearing guard whose modification requires elevated review; the tag is applied DELIBERATELY per test — blanket-tagging voids the discipline. CODEOWNERS-REQUIRED-REVIEWER GATE: the consuming project’s CODEOWNERS declares that modifications to [PROTECTED-SPEC]-tagged tests or a regression/**/* path scope require a designated required-reviewer approval BEFORE merge, composing §2.1 SSH + §11.4.113 no-force-push so the gate cannot be silently bypassed, with the audit trail in the review thread and commit history. The gate CATCHES the change so a reviewer can distinguish STRENGTHEN (approve) from FIX-DEFECT-IN-TEST (approve after verifying ATM-NNN’s defect is not silently re-introduced) from WEAKEN/REMOVE (refuse, or escalate per §11.4.66) — it makes modification VISIBLE, not impossible. Composes §2.1 / §11.4.6 / §11.4.35 / §11.4.50 (an empty quarantine is the ideal) / §11.4.54 / §11.4.66 / §11.4.113 / §11.4.115 / §11.4.135 / §11.4.197 / §11.4.226 / §1.1. Classification: universal (§11.4.17). Propagation gate CM-COVENANT-114-248-PROPAGATION (literal 11.4.248) + recommended gates CM-FLAKY-TEST-QUARANTINE-WIRED / CM-PROTECTED-REGRESSION-SPEC-GATE + paired §1.1 mutations (gate-code = separate work item, NOT claimed shipped §11.4.6/§11.4.227). Canonical authority: constitution submodule Constitution.md §11.4.248 — the full mandate text is canonical there and is inherited by reference (CONST-059 / §11.4.35). §11.4.249 — Producer ≠ oracle ≠ gate ≠ verifier + flight recorder (research-derived, 2026-08-15). Cascade reference (short form): every load-bearing quality mechanism has FOUR distinct roles that MUST be structurally separate — (1) PRODUCER (the code computing/emitting/building the output); (2) ORACLE (the mechanism deciding correctness per §11.4.245); (3) GATE (the seam consuming the oracle’s verdict to allow/block/proceed); (4) VERIFIER (the mechanism auditing the gate’s decision — the paired §1.1 mutation proving the gate CAN refuse a bad output). Collapsing any two produces a specific bluff class: producer=oracle (the §11.4.245 oracle-agrees-with-code failure); oracle=gate (an oracle that cannot say “cannot decide” defaults to allow — the §11.4.201(6) FALSE-NULL); gate=verifier (a self-auditing gate — the §11.4.142 self-review anti-pattern applied). FLIGHT RECORDER: a mechanism CAPTURING producer inputs + oracle verdict + gate decision + verifier audit as a durable, replayable artifact (a structured JSONL event stream per §11.4.116, content-addressed per §11.4.207) so the post-hoc question “why did this go through” has evidence rather than a shrug; the recorder is NOT the gate — a gate reading its own recorder to decide collapses gate + verifier + recorder. DETECTABLE COLLAPSES the §11.4.194 reviewer scans for: (1) producer=oracle (the test’s assertion calls the code being tested); (2) oracle=gate (no “cannot decide” branch, defaults PASS); (3) gate=verifier (the meta-test IS the gate — a mutation making the gate always-pass ALSO makes the meta-test always-pass; the meta-test MUST live OUTSIDE the gate); (4) recorder=decision-substrate (the gate uses recorder contents as a decision input — precedent-based gating is not authorised). Canon records a MATERIAL-THIN note: the four-role taxonomy is generalised from the producer/verifier separation + oracle-problem discussion + gate mechanics, and the FLIGHT RECORDER is derived from the clean-checkout re-run + attached-receipts pattern per §11.4.17. Composes §11.4.5 / §11.4.6 / §11.4.107(10) / §11.4.116 / §11.4.142 / §11.4.194 / §11.4.201 / §11.4.207 / §11.4.240 (the separation PRINCIPLE — §11.4.249 is the four-role taxonomy that instantiates it; the two mutually compose) / §11.4.245 / §1.1. Classification: universal (§11.4.17). Propagation gate CM-COVENANT-114-249-PROPAGATION (literal 11.4.249) + recommended gates CM-ROLE-SEPARATION-DECLARED / CM-FLIGHT-RECORDER-CAPTURED + paired §1.1 mutations (gate-code = separate work item, NOT claimed shipped §11.4.6/§11.4.227). Canonical authority: constitution submodule Constitution.md §11.4.249 — the full mandate text is canonical there and is inherited by reference (CONST-059 / §11.4.35). Non-compliance is a release blocker. No escape hatch — no --collapse-producer-oracle, --gate-self-audits, --skip-flight-recorder, --oracle-defaults-pass, --decision-without-recorded-rationale flag.

§11.4.250 — Heuristic-tower signals a primitive defect: when N heuristic layers stack to compensate, the primitive is broken (research-derived, 2026-08-15). Cascade reference (short form): a codebase accumulating a TOWER of heuristics all compensating for the same underlying issue (retry-with-backoff + connection-pool-warmup + DNS-cache-flush + health-check-with-startup-grace, all guarding one flaky DB connection) IS the diagnostic — the PRIMITIVE is defective and every layer papers over a manifestation without fixing the cause. RECOGNITION SIGNALS: (i) each layer’s justification is “we added this because sometimes X happens” — reactive; (ii) removing any single layer INCREASES failures, yet keeping all of them still shows an occasional residual; (iii) no single layer owns responsibility; (iv) the layers were added at different times by different authors per specific incidents; (v) monotone layer-count growth over time — a debugging session for the residual adds layer N+1 rather than fixing 1..N. DISTINCT FROM DEFENSE-IN-DEPTH: defense-in-depth is deliberate, each layer targets a DIFFERENT failure class, the layers are designed together rather than accreted, and removing one produces a SPECIFIC different failure rather than “the same failure more often”. SYSTEMATIC-DEBUGGING RESPONSE at tower depth ≥ 2: (1) STOP adding layers; (2) INVOKE §11.4.102 systematic-debugging on the PRIMITIVE; (3) work the root-cause / pattern / hypothesis / implementation phases; (4) fix the primitive; (5) REMOVE the layers one at a time, verifying each removal with §11.4.5 captured evidence; (6) commit each removal per §11.4.124 with git-history evidence citing the primitive fix. Composes §11.4.5 / §11.4.6 / §11.4.102 / §11.4.124 / §11.4.194 / §11.4.201 (an outer layer masks the §11.4.201(6) false-null) / §11.4.226 / §1.1. Classification: universal (§11.4.17). Propagation gate CM-COVENANT-114-250-PROPAGATION (literal 11.4.250) + recommended gate CM-HEURISTIC-TOWER-TRIGGERS-PRIMITIVE-DEBUG + paired §1.1 mutation (gate-code = separate work item, NOT claimed shipped §11.4.6/§11.4.227). Canonical authority: constitution submodule Constitution.md §11.4.250 — the full mandate text is canonical there and is inherited by reference (CONST-059 / §11.4.35). Non-compliance is a release blocker. No escape hatch — no --add-another-heuristic-layer, --skip-primitive-debug, --tower-is-defense-in-depth-without-justification, --reactive-mitigation-OK, --layer-per-incident flag.

§11.4.251 — Byte-identical-fork prohibition + role-as-data-pack (research-derived, 2026-08-15). Cascade reference (short form): when a project’s needs would be met by TWO NEAR-IDENTICAL CODE COPIES differing only in configuration / data / role-specific parameters (prod + staging with hardcoded hostnames; reader + writer with different flag values; web + mobile with different feature flags), those copies MUST NOT be maintained as parallel forks — ONE code copy is retained and the role-specific values are extracted into a DATA PACK (role-config file / env-var set / capability descriptor / feature-flag bundle) that the one code consumes to instantiate as either role; canon’s remedy phrasing: “roles become data-only packs you reference, never repos you fork”. DETECTION: a reviewer or mechanical scan finds a byte-identical fork when diff shows every difference is a configuration value (hostname/port/flag/label/env/credential-ref) rather than behavioural, a common git-history ancestor exists, and byte identity exceeds the project’s §11.4.35 threshold (canon’s illustrative figure: > 90%). EXTRACTION: (1) IDENTIFY the configuration surface; (2) EXTRACT it to a per-role data pack; (3) REFACTOR the single code copy to consume it at instantiation (constructor injection / factory / config-loader-at-startup); (4) DELETE one fork; (5) VERIFY per §11.4.5 that both role instantiations produce the same observable behaviour their forks previously produced (§11.4.243 characterization before refactor); (6) commit the extraction per §11.4.124 with git-history evidence. LEGITIMATE FORKS: a temporary migration (tracked §11.4.197 end date), an experimental / A-B fork (bounded end date), or genuinely divergent behaviour (copies differing in algorithm / protocol / data model, not merely config). Composes §11.4.5 / §11.4.6 / §11.4.28 / §11.4.35 / §11.4.124 / §11.4.177 / §11.4.194 / §11.4.197 / §11.4.243 / §1.1. Classification: universal (§11.4.17). Propagation gate CM-COVENANT-114-251-PROPAGATION (literal 11.4.251) + recommended gate CM-NO-BYTE-IDENTICAL-FORK + paired §1.1 mutation (gate-code = separate work item, NOT claimed shipped §11.4.6/§11.4.227). Canonical authority: constitution submodule Constitution.md §11.4.251 — the full mandate text is canonical there and is inherited by reference (CONST-059 / §11.4.35). Non-compliance is a release blocker. No escape hatch — no --fork-for-role-OK, --skip-data-pack-extraction, --copy-and-modify-config, --duplicate-code-per-environment, --near-identical-forks-are-fine flag.

§11.4.252 — Fail-closed-on-dangerous-combination for mutating / credential surfaces (research-derived, 2026-08-15). Cascade reference (short form): every code path COMBINING ≥ 2 dangerous capabilities MUST FAIL CLOSED — refusing the operation unless every capability’s precondition is verifiably satisfied — and MUST NEVER fail open on ambiguous, missing or unresolvable input; this is the security-engineering generalisation of §11.4.201 guard-honesty applied where a false negative’s blast radius dwarfs the operator inconvenience of a false positive. DANGEROUS-COMBINATION TAXONOMY (canon records this 6-class set as a §11.4.17 closed-set formalisation of the fail-closed pattern’s shape, MATERIAL-THIN rather than a literal per-class enumeration): (1) MUTATION of a shared resource (DB / filesystem / container state / config); (2) UNTRUSTED INPUT (user string / network bytes / external env var / unverified file); (3) CREDENTIAL ACCESS (secret read / authenticated API call / signing-key use); (4) EXTERNAL SIDE EFFECT (email / webhook / MQ publish / payment endpoint); (5) SHELL / EXEC (subshell / binary with user-influenced args / template eval over user values); (6) IRREVERSIBLE (delete / drop / truncate — force-push already being absolutely forbidden per §11.4.113). Any path combining ≥ 2 is a dangerous combination and MUST fail closed. THE INVARIANT: (1) VERIFY every precondition (authorization + input shape + credential presence + target identity); (2) REFUSE when ANY is unverifiable, with an ERROR EXIT naming the specific unresolved precondition; (3) EMIT captured evidence of the refusal per §11.4.5; (4) NEVER default to allow / proceed / retry-with-relaxed on ambiguity — the §11.4.101 conservative-safe default is REFUSE. FAIL-OPEN ANTI-PATTERNS refused by name: catch { /* ignore */ }, credential = credential || default, if (!valid) { log("warn"); return proceed(input); }, target = user_input || default_target, and an unbounded retry { dangerous(); }. Distinct from graceful degradation (a cache miss returning a cache-miss response is legitimate and is not a dangerous combination). Composes §11.4.5 / §11.4.6 / §11.4.10 (the credentials specialisation) / §11.4.66 / §11.4.101 / §11.4.113 (its maximal instantiation) / §11.4.194 / §11.4.201 / §1.1. Classification: universal (§11.4.17). Propagation gate CM-COVENANT-114-252-PROPAGATION (literal 11.4.252) + recommended gate CM-DANGEROUS-COMBINATION-FAIL-CLOSED + paired §1.1 mutation (gate-code = separate work item, NOT claimed shipped §11.4.6/§11.4.227). Canonical authority: constitution submodule Constitution.md §11.4.252 — the full mandate text is canonical there and is inherited by reference (CONST-059 / §11.4.35). Non-compliance is a release blocker. No escape hatch — no --fail-open-on-error, --swallow-exception, --default-missing-credential, --proceed-on-ambiguous-input, --retry-until-succeeds flag.

§11.4.253 — Idempotency under retry + DB-level durable uniqueness guard (research-derived, 2026-08-15). Cascade reference (short form): every retryable operation (network calls with auto-retry, at-least-once MQ consumers, cron jobs with overlapping schedules, HTTP endpoints with client retry, DB transactions with retry-on-serialisation-failure, deploy pipelines with rerun capability) MUST BE IDEMPOTENT — N executions produce the same observable end state as one — and idempotency MUST be ENFORCED at the LOWEST LAYER where “same end state” is durably provable, typically a DATABASE UNIQUENESS CONSTRAINT on a caller-supplied idempotency key, NEVER only an application-layer check-then-insert (which races).
