Forensic anchor: FIFTEEN instances in one session where an ad-hoc measurement returned a confident, plausible, actionable result that was an artefact of the instrument, not a property of the system — a pattern matching only one of two block-opener forms (manufacturing 24 phantom findings); comm on numerically-sorted input reporting all 34 items missing; $? read after a pipeline turning exit 1 into 0; a field-name typo reading as “every record lost its key binding”; grep -w matching zai inside zai-coding-plan and corrupting a classification; a summary grep assuming passed, failed when failure prints failed, passed; while read dropping an unterminated final line so a one-element list iterated zero times; a mutation applied with the wrong env knob so the REAL tree was tested and the green meant nothing; a guard probe using a command the guard does not target, proving nothing while reading as a negative; a fixture whose two columns held the same string, so a real column-swap mutation survived at 52/0 under a comment asserting that could not happen; a positive control chosen OUTSIDE the instrument’s scope reporting a working instrument as broken, and another chosen to BE the question under test so it could not discriminate; an awk range whose escaping matched nothing reporting 838 rows where the system held 134, about to gate a DESTRUCTIVE recreate; a two-literal denylist searched with ONE literal — both loaded, both lengths printed, one searched — so a live credential was reported eliminated while still handed over in four copy-pasteable lines per file, with the positive control PASSING because it shared the same single needle; and $? clobbered by a command substitution in the same argument list, reporting a pandoc failure (exit 64) as exit 0. The gap: §11.4.201 governs guards/gates (instruments that BLOCK) and §11.4.107(10) governs analyzers (calibrated golden-good/bad); NEITHER covers the ad-hoc census that INFORMS — a grep, a set difference, an exit-code read, a field lookup — whose output is not a verdict but a FACT a decision is then built on, written inline, used once, trusted implicitly, never itself tested. Mandate: any measurement whose result will drive a decision, claim, classification or report MUST be control-needled BEFORE it is acted on — (a) a POSITIVE control (a value known present must be found: catches too-narrow patterns, wrong field names, wrong paths, wrong knobs, silently-empty stages); (b) a NEGATIVE control (a fabricated needle must NOT be found: catches too-broad patterns, accidental substring/word-boundary matches, fabricating defaults); (c) an EMPTY result is NEVER a finding alone — absent, could-not-look, looked-in-the-wrong-place and output-shape-differed are four distinct states the raw empty distinguishes between none of (§11.4.6); (d) a result CONTRADICTING directly-observed evidence is presumed an instrument fault until proven otherwise; (e) the instrument is part of the evidence — a captured claim cites the command that produced it (§11.4.5/§11.4.2); (f) when the criterion is a SET, the control must cover EVERY member — needling ONE member proves only that member is searchable, and (a) and (b) both PASS while the answer is wrong if the search and its control share the same single needle (measured: a two-literal denylist searched with one literal reported a live credential eliminated while it was still handed over in four copy-pasteable lines per file); (g) capture an exit status BEFORE any other expansion can overwrite it — $? is the LAST command’s status, which in a compound statement is often not the one being reported on, whether a pipeline’s last stage or a command substitution earlier in the same argument list. Honest boundary (§11.4.6): control needles prove an instrument can tell PRESENT from ABSENT; they do NOT prove it measures the RIGHT property, and a correctly-needled census of the wrong thing is still the wrong answer. Classification: universal (§11.4.17). Composes §11.4.5/.6/.2/.107(10)/.118/.201/§1.1. Propagation gate CM-COVENANT-114-273-PROPAGATION (literal 11.4.273) + recommended gate CM-CENSUS-CONTROL-NEEDLED + paired §1.1 mutation (gate-code = separate work item, NOT claimed shipped). Canonical authority: constitution submodule Constitution.md §11.4.273. Non-compliance is a release blocker. No escape hatch — no --skip-control-needle, --empty-is-absent, --trust-the-grep, --census-needs-no-proof, --obvious-result-exempt flag.

§11.4.274 — Mechanical work belongs in a script, not in an agent’s context: extract it, test it, and re-scan for it on a cadence (User mandate, 2026-09-08). Forensic anchor: in one session five review agents consumed ~400–450k tokens EACH, and the dominant cost was not judgment but MECHANICS — copy a tree to scratch, apply a one-line mutation, run a suite, read the summary, restore, verify byte-identical, compare against the previous round, repeat seven times. Every step is deterministic, fixed-input/fixed-output, decision-free: a shell script, executed instead one tool call at a time by a language model at orders of magnitude the cost, with a fresh chance of transcription error at each step (this session logged ten such instrument errors, §11.4.273). Rule: work that is DETERMINISTIC, REPEATABLE and contains NO JUDGMENT MUST be delegated to a properly written script or small compiled utility and invoked, not performed turn-by-turn — mutation apply/run/restore/verify loops, suite fan-outs and result tabulation, before/after comparisons, scratch setup and teardown, residue and quiescence scans, census and inventory queries, artifact fingerprinting, evidence collection and layout, and any step already written down as a fixed recipe. (a) EXTRACT: a mechanical sequence an agent performs more than twice is a defect in the tooling, not a task — name it, script it, invoke it. (b) THE TOOL IS GOVERNED: it ships with actionable documentation (§11.4.18), a user guide/manual entry where operator-facing, and tests validating its behaviour AND producing machine evidence deterministically (§11.4.50/§11.4.5) — including failure paths, exit-code semantics (§11.4.201) and the control needles proving its own measurements (§11.4.273); replacing a slow correct process with a fast unproven one is a regression, not an optimisation. (c) RE-SCAN ON A CADENCE: every project periodically scans itself for mechanical work still done by agents (recurring command sequences in transcripts/logs, repeated inline recipes, “run these commands in order” docs) and refactors the findings out — a standing obligation, since new mechanical loops accrete continuously. (d) HONEST BOUNDARY (§11.4.6) — what MUST NOT be extracted: JUDGMENT. Root-cause determination (§11.4.102), review verdicts (§11.4.125/§11.4.142/§11.4.194), the decision that a fix is correct, the choice of what to measure, the reading of whether evidence supports a claim, and any assessment requiring “is this the right thing to check?” A script that renders a verdict is a bluff gate (§11.4.201), and automating a review into a green checkmark is the exact PASS-bluff this constitution exists to prevent. The division is: the SCRIPT gathers and executes deterministically and cheaply; the AGENT decides what it means. Cost is NEVER a reason to move a decision into a script. Classification: universal (§11.4.17). Composes §11.4.18/.50/.5/.141/.201/.273/.124/§1.1. Propagation gate CM-COVENANT-114-274-PROPAGATION (literal 11.4.274) + recommended gates CM-MECHANICAL-WORK-EXTRACTED / CM-EXTRACTED-TOOL-DOCUMENTED-AND-TESTED + paired §1.1 mutation (gate-code = separate work item, NOT claimed shipped). Canonical authority: constitution submodule Constitution.md §11.4.274. Non-compliance is a release blocker. No escape hatch — no --skip-extraction, --agent-can-do-it, --one-off-is-fine, --script-the-review, --extract-without-tests flag.
